- **price-target-retracement-skill**: 用于回撤位与价格目标测算（计算类）：回撤位=波段 A→B 的 38%/50%/62% 三档（50% 最通用），须等蜡烛信号验证才可买卖（不在裸回撤位买卖）；目标四式=箱体突破（高度投影到被突破边外，失效=收市回箱体）、对等运动（第一波高度加到调整低点）、旗形（旗杆加到密集区底边，尼森保守算法，口径须声明）、三角形（最宽处高度加到被突破边）；目标只用于平仓不用于开仓。适用于"目标价看到哪""回调到哪买"。不适用于到位就买（目标≠支撑）、蜡烛图自身不预测幅度。触发词：目标价/price target/回撤位/retracement/斐波那契/Fibonacci/箱体突破/对等运动。 ([raphael-y7/jp-candlestick-analysis-skill](https://github.com/raphael-y7/jp-candlestick-analysis-skill/tree/HEAD/pack/price-target-retracement-skill))

- **price-the-door-before-you-enter**: A repo has thousands of open issues, a friendly CONTRIBUTING and an obvious defect you could fix, and none of that tells you whether a contribution can land. Open issues measure demand; the merge path measures supply. Before reading a single issue, price the door with four queries — open PRs and how many are stale, merged vs closed-unmerged over the same window, created-to-merged latency, and the author spread of recent merges — then read the licence and the CLA acceptance mechanism, because in some houses opening the PR *is* the signature.
